<script id="ethvelle-v283-handle-tap-forwarder">
/* v283 FIX ("tapping a clip's EDGE selects it but the scrubber does not move"): the trim handles
   (the ~20px grips at both ends of a clip) were excluded from the directional clip seek, and the
   overlay handle also swallows its pointer events in a window-capture listener. This tiny listener
   is registered FIRST (head), so it sees every handle tap before anything can stop it, and hands
   the gesture to the same seek logic a tap on the clip body uses (window.__ethvelleV283HandleSeek,
   defined by the v264 script). It never stops propagation, so trimming / long-press are untouched. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV283HandleTapForwarder)return;
  window.__ethvelleV283HandleTapForwarder=true;
  var HANDLE='.anim-clip-handle,[data-ov-handle],[data-seg-handle],[data-bg-handle]';
  var activeId=null;
  function api(){return window.__ethvelleV283HandleSeek||null;}
  function mark(e){try{e.__evV283Forwarded=true;}catch(_e){}}
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    activeId=null;
    var a=api();
    if(!a||e.button>0||!e.target||!e.target.closest)return;
    if(!e.target.closest('#timelineScroll')||!e.target.closest(HANDLE))return;
    activeId=e.pointerId;mark(e);
    try{a.pointerdown(e);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v283 handle tap:',err);}
  },true);
  ['pointermove','pointerup','pointercancel'].forEach(function(type){
    window.addEventListener(type,function(e){
      if(activeId===null||e.pointerId!==activeId)return;
      var a=api();mark(e);
      if(type!=='pointermove')activeId=null;
      if(!a)return;
      try{a[type](e);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v283 handle tap:',err);}
    },true);
  });
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v203-playback-diagnostics-core">
/* v203 diagnostics counters. Inert unless the page URL contains ?evdebug=1. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var enabled=false;
  try{enabled=new URLSearchParams(window.location.search||'').get('evdebug')==='1';}catch(_e){}
  window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics={
    enabled:enabled,
    startedAt:(window.performance&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now(),
    stopCounts:{drift:0,pause:0,start:0,edit:0,other:0,total:0},
    sourceNodeTimes:[],anchorRecreations:0,rebufferCount:0,kickCount:0,
    recordStop:function(reason){
      if(!enabled)return;
      var key=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(this.stopCounts,reason)?reason:'other';
      this.stopCounts[key]++;this.stopCounts.total++;
    },
    recordSourceNode:function(){
      if(!enabled)return;
      var playing=false;try{playing=typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&!!isPlaying;}catch(_e){}
      if(!playing)return;
      var now=(window.performance&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
      this.sourceNodeTimes.push(now);
      while(this.sourceNodeTimes.length&&now-this.sourceNodeTimes[0]>60000)this.sourceNodeTimes.shift();
    },
    recordAnchor:function(){if(enabled)this.anchorRecreations++;},
    recordRebuffer:function(){if(enabled)this.rebufferCount++;},
    recordKick:function(){if(enabled)this.kickCount++;}
  };
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== Ethvelle v189: early boot guard + main-thread budget (must stay first in <head>) ===== -->
<style id="ethvelle-v189-boot-guard-css">
  /* 1) Login-consent dialog + its logo are defined by CSS that sits at the very END of this
        3.7 MB file. While the browser was still parsing, the dialog was an unstyled block and
        its logo drew at natural (huge) size behind the fading splash. Define the essentials
        here so it can never show before its real styles arrive. */
  .ev-login-consent-backdrop{display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483000;align-items:center;justify-content:center;}
  .ev-login-consent-backdrop.active{display:flex!important;}
  .ev-login-consent-mark{width:82px;height:62px;overflow:hidden;}
  .ev-login-consent-mark img{display:block;width:82px;height:62px;max-width:100%;object-fit:contain;}
  /* 2) Until the whole document (and therefore every <style> block) has been parsed, only the
        splash is allowed to paint. Removed on DOMContentLoaded (with a safety timeout). */
  html.ev-booting #app>*:not(#screen-splash){visibility:hidden!important;}
  html.ev-booting body>:not(#app):not(script):not(style):not(link):not(meta){visibility:hidden!important;}
  /* Any large brand image can never exceed its slot while booting. */
  html.ev-booting .admin-login-logo,html.ev-booting .home-brand-logo-only{max-width:180px;max-height:96px;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v199-decoder-budget">
/* v203: mobile defaults to three decoders (two warm slots). The opt-in URL flag
   ?evdecoders=4 raises the pool for devices that can sustain extra prewarming. */
(function(){
  try{
    var mem=Number(navigator.deviceMemory)||4,cores=Number(navigator.hardwareConcurrency)||4;
    var mobile=/Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobi/i.test(navigator.userAgent||'');
    var pool=3;
    if(!mobile){if(mem>=4&&cores>=4)pool=4;if(mem>=8&&cores>=8)pool=5;}
    var forceFour=false;
    try{forceFour=new URLSearchParams(window.location.search||'').get('evdecoders')==='4';}catch(_e){}
    if(forceFour)pool=4;
    window.__evDecoderBudget={pool:pool,warm:pool-1};
  }catch(_e){window.__evDecoderBudget={pool:3,warm:2};}
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v189-boot-guard-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var root=document.documentElement;
  root.classList.add('ev-booting');
  var done=false;
  function reveal(){
    if(done)return;done=true;
    // One frame later so the last parsed <style> has been applied before anything is shown.
    var go=function(){root.classList.remove('ev-booting');};
    if(window.requestAnimationFrame)requestAnimationFrame(function(){requestAnimationFrame(go);});else go();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',reveal,{once:true});else reveal();
  window.addEventListener('load',reveal,{once:true});
  setTimeout(function(){done=false;reveal();},4000); // fail-safe: never leave the app hidden
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v189-observer-budget-js">
/* The editor registers ~100 MutationObservers (many on document.body with subtree:true).
   Selecting a text/audio layer toggles classes in several places, so every one of them ran in
   the same task and froze the UI. This wrapper keeps their behaviour, but:
     - merges all records that arrive within a frame into ONE callback per observer,
     - delivers them just before the next paint (so fix-ups never flash on screen),
     - spends at most ~6 ms per frame on observer callbacks; the rest roll to the next frame.  */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var Native=window.MutationObserver;
  if(!Native||Native.__ev189)return;
  var queue=[],scheduled=false,BUDGET=6;
  function now(){return (window.performance&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();}
  function schedule(){
    if(scheduled)return;scheduled=true;
    var run=function(){if(!scheduled)return;scheduled=false;pump();};
    if(window.requestAnimationFrame)requestAnimationFrame(run);
    setTimeout(run,100); // background tabs don't fire rAF
  }
  /* v220: while the playhead is being scrubbed, every DOM change the scrub itself causes used to
     wake all text-panel observers each frame (slider/palette/toolbar re-scans), which made sliding
     laggy with a text layer selected. During a scrub each observer now gets at most one merged
     delivery per SCRUB_GAP ms; whatever is still held is delivered right after the scrub ends. */
  var SCRUB_GAP=260,held=[],holdTimer=0;
  function releaseHeld(){
    holdTimer=0;
    var list=held;held=[];
    for(var i=0;i<list.length;i++){var h=list[i];h._held=false;if(!h._off&&h._pending.length&&!h._q){h._q=true;queue.push(h);}}
    if(queue.length)schedule();
  }
  function pump(){
    var t0=now();
    while(queue.length){
      var w=queue.shift();w._q=false;
      if(window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing&&now()-(w._last||0)<SCRUB_GAP){
        if(w._pending.length>2000)w._pending=w._pending.slice(-2000);
        if(!w._held){w._held=true;held.push(w);}
        if(!holdTimer)holdTimer=setTimeout(releaseHeld,SCRUB_GAP/2);
        continue;
      }
      w._last=now();w._deliver();
      if(queue.length&&now()-t0>BUDGET){schedule();return;}
    }
  }
  function Wrapped(cb){
    var self=this;
    this._cb=cb;this._pending=[];this._q=false;this._off=false;
    this._inner=new Native(function(records){
      if(self._off)return;
      for(var i=0;i<records.length;i++)self._pending.push(records[i]);
      if(!self._q&&!self._held){self._q=true;queue.push(self);schedule();}
    });
  }
  Wrapped.prototype._deliver=function(){
    if(this._off||!this._pending.length)return;
    var recs=this._pending;this._pending=[];
    try{this._cb.call(this,recs,this);}catch(e){try{console.error(e);}catch(_e){}}
  };
  Wrapped.prototype.observe=function(target,options){this._off=false;return this._inner.observe(target,options);};
  Wrapped.prototype.disconnect=function(){this._off=true;this._pending=[];return this._inner.disconnect();};
  Wrapped.prototype.takeRecords=function(){var r=this._pending.concat(this._inner.takeRecords());this._pending=[];return r;};
  Wrapped.__ev189=true;
  try{window.MutationObserver=Wrapped;}catch(_e){}
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== Ethvelle v191: text-panel sliders. Registered before every other window listener so a
     swipe that starts anywhere on a slider row (not only on the 14px thumb) drags the value in ONE
     touch; a vertical swipe on the same row scrolls the panel natively. ===== -->
<script id="ethvelle-v191-text-slider-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV191TextSliders)return;
  window.__ethvelleV191TextSliders=true;

  /* Text-editing panels only. Every other slider in the app keeps its existing behaviour. */
  var SCOPE='#ethvellePicsartTextDock,#ethvelleTextStylePanel,#ethvelleTextComposer,#evpaCustomEffectEditor,#ethvelleColorPicker,#textPanelBody';
  /* One "row" = caption + slider. The whole row is the grab area, not just the 14px thumb. */
  var ROWS='.evpa-range,.evts-field,.evtc-opacity-line,.evce-field,.evcp-field,.evpa-picker-range,.evce-stop,.text-range-line';
  /* Things that own their own touches (buttons, scrolling chip rows, colour wheel...). */
  var BLOCK='button,select,textarea,a,input:not([type="range"]),[contenteditable="true"],#evpaTabs,'+
            '.evpa-font-gallery,.evpa-style-presets,.evpa-gradient-presets,.evpa-choice-row,.evpa-swatches,'+
            '.evcp-wheel,.evpa-hue-ring,.evpa-sat-square,canvas';
  var THUMB=14;          // visible thumb width in the text-panel CSS
  var SLOP_X=5;          // px of sideways travel that starts a value drag
  var SLOP_Y=8;          // px of vertical travel that hands the gesture to native scrolling
  var st=null;           // active gesture

  function num(v,d){var n=parseFloat(v);return isFinite(n)?n:d;}
  function usable(input){
    var r=input.getBoundingClientRect();
    return {r:r,w:Math.max(24,r.width-THUMB)};
  }
  function okInput(i){
    if(!i||i.disabled)return false;
    var r=i.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width>24&&r.height>0;
  }
  /* Which slider does a touch at (x,y) on element t belong to? */
  function pickRange(t,x,y){
    if(t.matches&&t.matches('input[type="range"]'))return okInput(t)?t:null;
    var scope=t.closest(SCOPE);if(!scope)return null;
    var list=[],row=t.closest(ROWS);
    if(row&&scope.contains(row))list=[].slice.call(row.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]'));
    if(!list.length){
      /* No known row wrapper: fall back to a finger-sized band around each slider. */
      list=[].slice.call(scope.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]')).filter(function(i){
        var r=i.getBoundingClientRect();
        return x>=r.left-10&&x<=r.right+10&&Math.abs(y-(r.top+r.height/2))<=Math.max(18,r.height/2+10);
      });
    }
    var best=null,bd=1e9;
    for(var k=0;k<list.length;k++){
      var i=list[k];if(!okInput(i))continue;
      var r=i.getBoundingClientRect(),d=Math.abs(y-(r.top+r.height/2));
      if(d<bd){bd=d;best=i;}
    }
    return best;
  }
  function valueAt(s,x){
    var min=s.min,max=s.max,v=s.startVal+((x-s.x0)/s.w)*(max-min);
    if(s.step>0)v=min+Math.round((v-min)/s.step)*s.step;
    v=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v));
    var dec=(String(s.input.step||'1').split('.')[1]||'').length;
    return String(Number(v.toFixed(Math.min(7,dec))));
  }
  function push(s,x,type){
    var inp=s.input;if(!inp.isConnected)return;
    var next=valueAt(s,x);
    if(inp.value!==next){inp.value=next;inp.dispatchEvent(new Event(type||'input',{bubbles:true}));}
  }
  function finish(s,x){
    if(s.raf){cancelAnimationFrame(s.raf);s.raf=0;}
    if(s.mode!=='value')return;
    push(s,x==null?s.lastX:x,'input');
    if(s.input.isConnected)s.input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
  }
  function stopAll(e){
    if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    else if(e.stopPropagation)e.stopPropagation();
  }

  function onDown(e){
    if(st){
      if(e.pointerId!==st.id)return;      // ignore a second finger
      st=null;                             // stale gesture with the same id
    }
    if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return;
    var t=e.target;if(!t||t.nodeType!==1||!t.closest)return;
    if(!t.closest(SCOPE))return;
    if(t.closest(BLOCK))return;
    var input=pickRange(t,e.clientX,e.clientY);
    if(!input)return;
    var g=usable(input);
    var min=num(input.min,0),max=num(input.max,100);
    if(!(max>min))return;
    var stepAttr=input.step,step=stepAttr==='any'?0:(num(stepAttr,1)||1);
    /* Self-heal: if some other script re-enabled native input on this slider, switch it off again. */
    try{input.style.setProperty('pointer-events','none','important');input.style.setProperty('touch-action','pan-y','important');}catch(_e){}
    st={id:e.pointerId,input:input,x0:e.clientX,y0:e.clientY,lastX:e.clientX,
        startVal:num(input.value,min),min:min,max:max,step:step,w:g.w,
        mode:'pending',raf:0,down:e};
    /* Keep the canvas / sheet gesture engines out of a slider touch (same isolation as before). */
    stopAll(e);
  }
  function onMove(e){
    var s=st;if(!s||e.pointerId!==s.id)return;
    var dx=e.clientX-s.x0,dy=e.clientY-s.y0,ax=Math.abs(dx),ay=Math.abs(dy);
    if(s.mode==='pending'){
      if(ax>=SLOP_X&&ax>ay*1.25){
        s.mode='value';
        /* Legacy "slider started" hook (e.g. inline onpointerdown on some sliders). */
        try{if(typeof s.input.onpointerdown==='function')s.input.onpointerdown.call(s.input,s.down);}catch(err){}
      }else if(ay>=SLOP_Y&&ay>=ax*0.8){
        st=null;                            // vertical swipe: let the panel scroll, value untouched
        return;
      }else return;
    }
    s.lastX=e.clientX;
    stopAll(e);
    if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
    if(!s.raf)s.raf=requestAnimationFrame(function(){s.raf=0;if(st===s)push(s,s.lastX,'input');});
  }
  function onUp(e){
    var s=st;if(!s||e.pointerId!==s.id)return;
    st=null;
    if(s.mode==='value'){
      if(isFinite(e.clientX))s.lastX=e.clientX;
      stopAll(e);
      finish(s,s.lastX);
    }
    /* mode==='pending' is a plain tap: nothing changes (no track-jump). */
  }
  function onCancel(e){
    var s=st;if(!s||e.pointerId!==s.id)return;
    st=null;
    if(s.mode==='value')finish(s,s.lastX);   // keep what the user dragged to
  }

  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',onDown,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointermove',onMove,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',onUp,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',onCancel,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('blur',function(){var s=st;st=null;if(s&&s.mode==='value')finish(s,s.lastX);});
  window.__ethvelleV191TextSlidersDebug=function(){return st;};
})();
</script>
<!-- ============================================================================
     Ethvelle v176 — thumb-only sliders everywhere in the clip editor, a professional
     crop frame, and a lag-free crop gesture engine.

     Why this block lives at the very top of <head>:
     window capture listeners fire in registration order. Registering first lets the
     slider and crop engines claim their gestures BEFORE the many older document/window
     handlers (character drag, selection box, canvas gestures, older slider engines)
     get a chance to run heavy work on every pointer event.
     ============================================================================ -->
<style id="ethvelle-v176-pro-crop-css">

  #evProCropLayer path{fill:rgba(7,9,15,.66);fill-rule:evenodd;}

    position:absolute;left:0;top:0;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:99px;white-space:nowrap;

    font:700 11px/1 Inter,system-ui,-apple-system,"Segoe UI",sans-serif;letter-spacing:.02em;
    font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;opacity:0;transition:opacity .14s ease;will-change:transform;
  }
  #evProCropLayer.evpc-drag #evProCropBadge{opacity:1;}

  /* ---- Frame: hairline white border, no blue slab, no giant shadow ---- */
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.ev-v89-crop-root{--evpc-inv:1;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame.ev-v89-crop-frame{

    pointer-events:none!important;cursor:default!important;transition:none!important;
  }

  }
  /* Rule-of-thirds grid: hidden until the user is dragging, then fades in. */
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame.ev-v89-crop-frame::before{
    content:""!important;position:absolute!important;left:0!important;right:0!important;top:0!important;bottom:0!important;

      linear-gradient(to right,transparent calc(33.333% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(33.333% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(33.333% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),transparent calc(33.333% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1))),
      linear-gradient(to right,transparent calc(66.666% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(66.666% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(66.666% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),transparent calc(66.666% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1))),
      linear-gradient(to bottom,transparent calc(33.333% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(33.333% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(33.333% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),transparent calc(33.333% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1))),
      linear-gradient(to bottom,transparent calc(66.666% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(66.666% - .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),rgba(255,255,255,.62) calc(66.666% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)),transparent calc(66.666% + .5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)))!important;
  }
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame.ev-v89-crop-frame::before{opacity:1;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame.ev-v89-crop-frame::after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}

  /* ---- Handles: L-shaped corner brackets + short edge bars (pure CSS, scale-compensated) ---- */
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle{
    position:absolute!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;min-width:0!important;min-height:0!important;
    margin:0!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;background:transparent!important;
    box-shadow:none!important;transform:none!important;pointer-events:none!important;z-index:3!important;
  }
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle::after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle::before{
    content:"";position:absolute;display:block;box-sizing:border-box;background:transparent;
  }
  /* anchor points */
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="nw"]{left:0!important;top:0!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="ne"]{left:100%!important;top:0!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="se"]{left:100%!important;top:100%!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="sw"]{left:0!important;top:100%!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="n"]{left:50%!important;top:0!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="s"]{left:50%!important;top:100%!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="w"]{left:0!important;top:50%!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="e"]{left:100%!important;top:50%!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
  /* corner brackets */
  .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="nw"]::before{left:calc(-1.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));top:calc(-1.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));width:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));height:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));border-top:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-left:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-top-left-radius:calc(3px * var(--evpc-inv,1));}
  .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="ne"]::before{left:calc(-20.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));top:calc(-1.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));width:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));height:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));border-top:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-right:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-top-right-radius:calc(3px * var(--evpc-inv,1));}
  .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="se"]::before{left:calc(-20.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));top:calc(-20.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));width:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));height:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));border-bottom:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-right:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-bottom-right-radius:calc(3px * var(--evpc-inv,1));}
  .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="sw"]::before{left:calc(-1.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));top:calc(-20.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1));width:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));height:calc(22px * var(--evpc-inv,1));border-bottom:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-left:calc(3.5px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) solid #fff;border-bottom-left-radius:calc(3px * var(--evpc-inv,1));}
  /* edge bars */
  .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="n"]::before,.ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="s"]::before{left:calc(-15px * var(--evpc-inv,1));top:calc(-2px * var(--evpc-inv,1));width:calc(30px * var(--evpc-inv,1));height:calc(4px * var(--evpc-inv,1));background:#fff;border-radius:calc(2px * var(--evpc-inv,1));box-shadow:0 0 0 calc(.75px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) rgba(0,0,0,.35);}
  .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="w"]::before,.ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="e"]::before{left:calc(-2px * var(--evpc-inv,1));top:calc(-15px * var(--evpc-inv,1));width:calc(4px * var(--evpc-inv,1));height:calc(30px * var(--evpc-inv,1));background:#fff;border-radius:calc(2px * var(--evpc-inv,1));box-shadow:0 0 0 calc(.75px * var(--evpc-inv,1)) rgba(0,0,0,.35);}
  /* Only the handle being touched turns (deep) blue, and only while the finger is down. */
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="nw"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="ne"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="se"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="sw"]::before{border-color:#2F5CE8;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="n"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="s"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="w"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.evpc-active #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle.evpc-hot[data-handle="e"]::before{background:#2F5CE8;}
  /* v178: crop corner brackets + edge bars are PERMANENTLY blue from the moment Crop opens (no touch needed). */
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="nw"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="ne"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="se"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="sw"]::before{border-color:#2F5CE8!important;}
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="n"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="s"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="w"]::before,
  html body #screen-editor #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot #evV89CropFrame .ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="e"]::before{background:#2F5CE8!important;}
  /* The "753 x 753" size pill under the crop border is removed. */
  #evProCropBadge{display:none!important;}
  /* The old full-stage shadow scrim is gone; the SVG layer above dims the outside instead. */
  #screen-editor.ev-v89-crop-active #canvasStage{touch-action:none!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v176-input-gate">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ev176Gate)return;
  const G=window.__ev176Gate={};
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const num=(v,d)=>{const n=parseFloat(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n:d;};
  const now=()=>(window.performance&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  let internal=0; // >0 while WE dispatch input/change so our own events pass through the value guard

  /* =====================================================================
     A. THUMB-ONLY SLIDERS
        A slider value changes ONLY while the finger/mouse is dragging the round
        thumb. Tapping or touching the rail does nothing. The text-editing, Transform,
        Speed, Volume and Audio sliders already behave this way and are left alone.
     ===================================================================== */
  const SKIP=[
    '#ethvellePicsartTextDock','#ethvelleTextStylePanel','#ethvelleTextComposer','#evpaCustomEffectEditor',
    '#ethvelleColorPicker','#textPanelBody',                       // text editing (already done)
    '.evtr-ruler','.transform-slider',                             // transform
    '#speedPanelBody','#volumePanelBody',                          // speed / volume
    '#evcaAudioPanel','.evca-audio-panel',                         // audio
    '#evcaInlineZoomHud'                                           // canvas zoom HUD
  ].join(',');
  function eligible(el){
    if(!el||el.nodeType!==1||el.tagName!=='INPUT'||el.type!=='range'||el.disabled)return false;
    try{return !el.closest(SKIP);}catch(_e){return false;}
  }
  let guard=null,guardTimer=0,drag=null;
  function setGuard(input){clearTimeout(guardTimer);guard={input,value:String(input.value)};}
  function releaseGuardSoon(){clearTimeout(guardTimer);guardTimer=setTimeout(()=>{guard=null;},180);}

  function geo(input){
    const r=input.getBoundingClientRect(),cs=getComputedStyle(input);
    let tw=0;
    try{
      const t=getComputedStyle(input,'::-webkit-slider-thumb');
      tw=parseFloat(t.width)||0;
      if(tw&&t.boxSizing!=='border-box')tw+=(parseFloat(t.borderLeftWidth)||0)+(parseFloat(t.borderRightWidth)||0)+(parseFloat(t.paddingLeft)||0)+(parseFloat(t.paddingRight)||0);
    }catch(_e){}
    if(!tw)try{tw=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(input,'::-moz-range-thumb').width)||0;}catch(_e){}
    // Chrome / Android WebView do NOT resolve getComputedStyle(input,'::-webkit-slider-thumb') and
    // return the WHOLE input width (e.g. "304px"). Trusting it collapsed the usable track to ~1px,
    // so any 2px touch jumped straight to min / max (100 -> 200). Accept only a plausible thumb
    // width, otherwise use the real thumb size (10px dot + 2x2px ring).
    if(!(tw>=8&&tw<=36&&tw<=r.width/4))tw=12;
    const half=tw/2;
    const pl=parseFloat(cs.paddingLeft)||0,pr=parseFloat(cs.paddingRight)||0;
    const left=r.left+pl+half,right=Math.max(left+1,r.right-pr-half);
    const min=num(input.min,0),max=num(input.max,100),val=num(input.value,min);
    const rtl=cs.direction==='rtl';
    let f=max===min?0:clamp((val-min)/(max-min),0,1);
    if(rtl)f=1-f;
    return {r,left,right,min,max,rtl,half,step:input.step==='any'?0:(Number(input.step)||1),x:left+f*(right-left),y:r.top+r.height/2};
  }
  // Finger-friendly circle around the visible thumb. Grabbing anywhere inside it keeps the
  // value unchanged until you actually drag (no jump), so a generous radius is safe.
  function near(g,x,y){
    return Math.abs(x-g.x)<=Math.max(20,g.half+12)&&Math.abs(y-g.y)<=Math.max(20,g.r.height/2+9);
  }
  function setX(input,x,g){
    let f=clamp((x-g.left)/(g.right-g.left),0,1);
    if(g.rtl)f=1-f;
    let v=g.min+f*(g.max-g.min);
    if(g.step>0)v=g.min+Math.round((v-g.min)/g.step)*g.step;
    v=clamp(v,g.min,g.max);
    const dec=(String(input.step||'1').split('.')[1]||'').length;
    const next=String(Number(v.toFixed(Math.min(7,dec))));
    if(input.value!==next){
      internal++;
      try{input.value=next;if(guard&&guard.input===input)guard.value=String(input.value);input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}
      finally{internal--;}
    }
  }
  function finishSlider(){
    const d=drag;if(!d)return;drag=null;
    if(d.raf){cancelAnimationFrame(d.raf);d.raf=0;}
    if(d.moved&&d.input.isConnected){
      setX(d.input,d.x-d.offset,d.g);
      internal++;try{d.input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}finally{internal--;}
    }
    try{d.input.releasePointerCapture(d.id);}catch(_e){}
    releaseGuardSoon();
  }
  function sliderPointer(e){
    const t=e.type;
    if(t==='pointerdown'){
      const input=e.target;
      if(!eligible(input))return false;
      if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return false;
      setGuard(input);
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
      const g=geo(input);
      if(!near(g,e.clientX,e.clientY))return true;            // rail tap: swallow, value untouched
      drag={id:e.pointerId,input,g,offset:e.clientX-g.x,startX:e.clientX,x:e.clientX,moved:false,raf:0,down:e};
      try{input.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      return true;
    }
    if((t==='pointerup'||t==='pointercancel')&&guard&&!drag)releaseGuardSoon();
    const d=drag;
    if(!d||d.id!==e.pointerId)return false;
    if(t==='pointermove'){
      d.x=e.clientX;
      if(!d.moved){
        if(Math.abs(d.x-d.startX)<2)return true;
        d.moved=true;d.offset=d.x-d.g.x;   // continue from the current value: no hop after the dead-zone
        // Keep legacy "slider started" hooks (e.g. character Position slider) working.
        try{if(typeof d.input.onpointerdown==='function')d.input.onpointerdown.call(d.input,d.down);}catch(err){console.warn('Slider start hook:',err);}
      }
      if(!d.raf)d.raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{d.raf=0;if(drag===d&&d.input.isConnected)setX(d.input,d.x-d.offset,d.g);});
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
      return true;
    }
    if(t==='pointerup'||t==='pointercancel'||t==='lostpointercapture'){
      if(Number.isFinite(e.clientX)&&t==='pointerup')d.x=e.clientX;
      finishSlider();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function sliderTouch(e){
    const t=e.target,type=e.type;
    if(type==='touchstart'){
      if(!eligible(t))return false;
      setGuard(t);
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();   // stops the browser's native "tap the rail to jump"
      return true;
    }
    if(type==='touchmove'){
      if(drag&&eligible(t)){if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();return true;}
      return false;
    }
    if((type==='touchend'||type==='touchcancel')&&guard&&eligible(t))releaseGuardSoon();
    return false;
  }
  function sliderMouse(e){
    if(!eligible(e.target))return false;
    if(e.type==='mousedown')setGuard(e.target);
    if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
    return true;
  }
  // Safety net: if a native value change still sneaks through during a pointer sequence that
  // was not our own thumb drag, put the old value back and hide the event from the app.
  function sliderValue(e){
    if(internal>0)return false;
    const t=e.target;
    if(!guard||guard.input!==t||!eligible(t))return false;
    if(String(t.value)!==guard.value)t.value=guard.value;
    return true;
  }

  /* =====================================================================
     B. PRO CROP ENGINE
        Owns every touch on the stage while Crop is open. Work per pointer event is a
        couple of multiplications; DOM writes are batched to one per animation frame; the
        heavy character/held-object renderer is paused for the duration of the drag.
     ===================================================================== */
  let cdrag=null,layer=null,lastCropEnd=0,refreshRaf=0;
  window.__ev176CropBusy=false;

  function cropActive(){
    const scr=$('screen-editor'),root=$('evV89CropRoot');
    return !!(scr&&scr.classList.contains('ev-v89-crop-active')&&root&&$('evV89CropFrame')&&root.style.display!=='none');
  }
  function cropCtx(){
    const id=String(window.__ethvelleCropEditingId||((typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?selectedClip:''));
    if(!id)return null;
    let clip=null;
    try{clip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!clip)return null;
    const ovs=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
    const bgs=(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds))?backgrounds:[];
    let el=null,kind='';
    if(ovs.includes(clip)){
      kind='overlay';
      el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
      if(!el){
        const node=document.querySelector('.overlay-media-item[data-overlay-id="'+id.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"]');
        el=node?(node.querySelector('img,video,canvas')||node):null;
      }
    }else if(bgs.includes(clip)){kind='background';el=$('bgImgLayer');}
    if(!kind)return null;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return null;
    const sw=Math.max(1,stage.clientWidth),sh=Math.max(1,stage.clientHeight);
    const iw=Math.max(1,(el&&(el.videoWidth||el.naturalWidth||el.width))||Number(clip.sourceWidth)||sw);
    const ih=Math.max(1,(el&&(el.videoHeight||el.naturalHeight||el.height))||Number(clip.sourceHeight)||sh);
    const scale=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),w=iw*scale,h=ih*scale;
    return {id,clip,kind,el,stage,sw,sh,iw,ih,m:{x:(sw-w)/2,y:(sh-h)/2,w,h,aspect:iw/ih}};
  }
  function cropValues(clip){
    const raw=typeof window.ethvelleCropFractions==='function'?window.ethvelleCropFractions(clip.crop):(clip.crop||{});
    const c=v=>clamp(Number(v)||0,0,.92);
    return {left:c(raw.left),right:c(raw.right),top:c(raw.top),bottom:c(raw.bottom)};
  }
  function normalizeCrop(c){
    let left=clamp(c.left,0,.92),right=clamp(c.right,0,.92),top=clamp(c.top,0,.92),bottom=clamp(c.bottom,0,.92);
    if(left+right>.92){const f=.92/(left+right);left*=f;right*=f;}
    if(top+bottom>.92){const f=.92/(top+bottom);top*=f;bottom*=f;}
    return {left,right,top,bottom};
  }
  // Layer (scale / rotation / zoom) transform of the crop root, resolved once per gesture.
  function rootMatrix(root,W,H){
    const cs=getComputedStyle(root);
    let a=1,b=0,c=0,d=1,e=0,f=0;
    const raw=cs.transform;
    if(raw&&raw!=='none'){try{const m=new DOMMatrixReadOnly(raw);a=m.a;b=m.b;c=m.c;d=m.d;e=m.e;f=m.f;}catch(_e){}}
    let ox=W/2,oy=H/2;
    const o=String(cs.transformOrigin||'').split(' ').map(parseFloat);
    if(Number.isFinite(o[0]))ox=o[0];if(Number.isFinite(o[1]))oy=o[1];
    return {a,b,c,d,e,f,ox,oy,det:a*d-b*c};
  }
  const toStage=(M,x,y)=>({x:M.ox+M.a*(x-M.ox)+M.c*(y-M.oy)+M.e,y:M.oy+M.b*(x-M.ox)+M.d*(y-M.oy)+M.f});
  function fromStage(M,X,Y){
    if(Math.abs(M.det)<1e-9)return null;
    const dx=X-M.ox-M.e,dy=Y-M.oy-M.f;
    return {x:M.ox+(M.d*dx-M.c*dy)/M.det,y:M.oy+(-M.b*dx+M.a*dy)/M.det};
  }
  function ensureLayer(stage){
    if(layer&&layer.isConnected&&layer.parentNode===stage)return layer;
    if(layer&&layer.parentNode)layer.remove();
    layer=document.createElement('div');layer.id='evProCropLayer';
    layer.innerHTML='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" preserveAspectRatio="none"><path d=""/></svg><div id="evProCropBadge"></div>';
    stage.appendChild(layer);
    return layer;
  }
  function removeLayer(){if(layer&&layer.parentNode)layer.remove();layer=null;}
  function frameRect(ctx,c){
    const m=ctx.m;
    return {L:m.x+c.left*m.w,T:m.y+c.top*m.h,w:Math.max(1,m.w*(1-c.left-c.right)),h:Math.max(1,m.h*(1-c.top-c.bottom))};
  }
  function paintOverlay(ctx,r,c,M,k,showBadge,ratioLabel){
    const root=$('evV89CropRoot');if(!root)return;
    const lay=ensureLayer(ctx.stage),svg=lay.firstChild,path=svg.firstChild,badge=lay.lastChild;
    const W=ctx.sw,H=ctx.sh;
    const vb='0 0 '+W+' '+H;
    if(svg.getAttribute('viewBox')!==vb)svg.setAttribute('viewBox',vb);
    const P=[toStage(M,r.L,r.T),toStage(M,r.L+r.w,r.T),toStage(M,r.L+r.w,r.T+r.h),toStage(M,r.L,r.T+r.h)];
    let d='M0 0H'+W+'V'+H+'H0Z M';
    d+=P[0].x.toFixed(2)+' '+P[0].y.toFixed(2);
    for(let i=1;i<4;i++)d+='L'+P[i].x.toFixed(2)+' '+P[i].y.toFixed(2);
    d+='Z';
    path.setAttribute('d',d);
    root.style.setProperty('--evpc-inv',clamp(1/k,.05,40).toFixed(4));
    if(showBadge){
      const xs=P.map(p=>p.x),ys=P.map(p=>p.y);
      const cx=clamp((Math.min(...xs)+Math.max(...xs))/2,46,Math.max(46,W-46));
      let top=Math.min(...ys)-10,anchor='-100%';
      if(top<30){top=Math.max(...ys)+10;anchor='0%';}
      const text=Math.round(ctx.iw*(1-c.left-c.right))+' × '+Math.round(ctx.ih*(1-c.top-c.bottom))+(ratioLabel&&ratioLabel!=='free'?'  ·  '+ratioLabel:'');
      if(badge.textContent!==text)badge.textContent=text;
      badge.style.transform='translate('+cx.toFixed(1)+'px,'+clamp(top,4,H-4).toFixed(1)+'px) translate(-50%,'+anchor+')';
    }
  }
  function writeFrame(ctx,r){
    const frame=$('evV89CropFrame');if(!frame)return;
    const s=frame.style;
    s.left=r.L+'px';s.top=r.T+'px';s.width=r.w+'px';s.height=r.h+'px';
  }
  // Re-sync the dim layer / handle scale after anything else moves the frame (ratio chips, Reset…).
  function refreshOverlay(){
    refreshRaf=0;
    if(cdrag)return;
    clearHot();
    if(!cropActive())return;
    const ctx=cropCtx();if(!ctx)return;
    const root=$('evV89CropRoot');
    const M=rootMatrix(root,ctx.sw,ctx.sh),sr=ctx.stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    const sx=sr.width/ctx.sw||1,sy=sr.height/ctx.sh||1;
    const k=(Math.sqrt(Math.abs(M.det))||1)*(sx+sy)/2;
    const c=cropValues(ctx.clip);
    paintOverlay(ctx,frameRect(ctx,c),c,M,k,false);
  }
  function scheduleRefresh(){if(!refreshRaf)refreshRaf=requestAnimationFrame(refreshOverlay);}

  function interactive(el){
    try{return !!(el&&el.closest&&el.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a,[role="button"],#evcaInlineZoomHud'));}catch(_e){return false;}
  }
  function pickHandle(p,r,k){
    // p, r in crop-root local px. Tolerances are defined in SCREEN px so they feel identical at any zoom/scale.
    const L=r.L,T=r.T,R=r.L+r.w,B=r.T+r.h;
    const small=Math.min(r.w,r.h)*k;
    const cTol=clamp(small/2.6,16,30),eTol=clamp(small/3.6,10,22);
    const corners={nw:[L,T],ne:[R,T],se:[R,B],sw:[L,B]};
    let best='',bd=Infinity;
    for(const n in corners){const dd=Math.hypot(p.x-corners[n][0],p.y-corners[n][1])*k;if(dd<bd){bd=dd;best=n;}}
    if(bd<=cTol)return best;
    const tolL=eTol/k;
    const inX=p.x>=L-tolL&&p.x<=R+tolL,inY=p.y>=T-tolL&&p.y<=B+tolL;
    const cand=[];
    if(inY)cand.push(['w',Math.abs(p.x-L)],['e',Math.abs(p.x-R)]);
    if(inX)cand.push(['n',Math.abs(p.y-T)],['s',Math.abs(p.y-B)]);
    let edge='',ed=Infinity;
    cand.forEach(([n,dd])=>{if(dd<ed){ed=dd;edge=n;}});
    if(edge&&ed*k<=eTol)return edge;
    if(p.x>=L&&p.x<=R&&p.y>=T&&p.y<=B)return 'move';
    return '';
  }
  function cropStart(e){
    const ctx=cropCtx();if(!ctx)return false;
    const root=$('evV89CropRoot');
    const sr=ctx.stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    const sx=sr.width/ctx.sw||1,sy=sr.height/ctx.sh||1;
    const M=rootMatrix(root,ctx.sw,ctx.sh);
    const k=(Math.sqrt(Math.abs(M.det))||1)*(sx+sy)/2;
    const p=fromStage(M,(e.clientX-sr.left)/sx,(e.clientY-sr.top)/sy);
    if(!p){if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();return true;}
    const startCrop=cropValues(ctx.clip);
    const handle=pickHandle(p,frameRect(ctx,startCrop),k);
    if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
    if(!handle)return true;                       // touch outside the frame is swallowed, nothing moves
    const ratio=(document.querySelector('.ev-v89-ratio.active')||{dataset:{}}).dataset.ratio||'free';
    cdrag={id:e.pointerId,ctx,M,sx,sy,k,handle,ratio,startCrop,x0:e.clientX,y0:e.clientY,cx:e.clientX,cy:e.clientY,raf:0,moved:false};
    window.__ev176CropBusy=true;                 // pause the heavy stage renderer while dragging
    root.classList.add('evpc-active');
    if(handle!=='move'){try{const hot=root.querySelector('.ev-v89-crop-handle[data-handle="'+handle+'"]');if(hot)hot.classList.add('evpc-hot');}catch(_e){}}
    if(layer)layer.classList.add('evpc-drag');
    try{ctx.stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    return true;
  }
  // Remove every "touched" highlight (frame border + the touched handle). Called on release and
  // from several safety nets, so the blue can never get stuck on after the finger is lifted.
  function clearHot(){
    const root=$('evV89CropRoot');
    if(root){root.classList.remove('evpc-active');root.querySelectorAll('.evpc-hot').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('evpc-hot'));}
    if(layer)layer.classList.remove('evpc-drag');
  }
  function cropCompute(d){
    const ctx=d.ctx,M=d.M;
    const ux=(d.cx-d.x0)/d.sx,uy=(d.cy-d.y0)/d.sy;                 // finger delta in stage px
    const lx=(M.d*ux-M.c*uy)/M.det,ly=(-M.b*ux+M.a*uy)/M.det;      // → crop-root local px
    const dx=lx/Math.max(1,ctx.m.w),dy=ly/Math.max(1,ctx.m.h);
    const s=d.startCrop,c={left:s.left,right:s.right,top:s.top,bottom:s.bottom},h=d.handle;
    const w0=1-s.left-s.right,hh0=1-s.top-s.bottom,min=.04;
    if(h==='move'){
      c.left=clamp(s.left+dx,0,1-w0);c.right=1-w0-c.left;
      c.top=clamp(s.top+dy,0,1-hh0);c.bottom=1-hh0-c.top;
    }else if(d.ratio==='free'){
      if(h.includes('w'))c.left=clamp(s.left+dx,0,1-s.right-min);
      if(h.includes('e'))c.right=clamp(s.right-dx,0,1-s.left-min);
      if(h.includes('n'))c.top=clamp(s.top+dy,0,1-s.bottom-min);
      if(h.includes('s'))c.bottom=clamp(s.bottom-dy,0,1-s.top-min);
    }else{
      const parts=String(d.ratio).split(':').map(Number);
      const wanted=(parts[0]/parts[1])/ctx.m.aspect;
      if(!Number.isFinite(wanted)||wanted<=0)return s;
      if(h==='e'||h==='w'){
        let w=clamp(w0+(h==='e'?dx:-dx),min,1),height=clamp(w/wanted,min,1);w=height*wanted;
        if(h==='e')c.right=1-s.left-w;else c.left=1-s.right-w;
        c.top=(1-height)/2;c.bottom=1-height-c.top;
      }else if(h==='n'||h==='s'){
        let height=clamp(hh0+(h==='s'?dy:-dy),min,1),w=clamp(height*wanted,min,1);height=w/wanted;
        if(h==='s')c.bottom=1-s.top-height;else c.top=1-s.bottom-height;
        c.left=(1-w)/2;c.right=1-w-c.left;
      }else{
        const dirX=h.includes('w')?-1:1,dirY=h.includes('n')?-1:1;
        const wC=clamp(w0+dirX*dx,min,1),hC=clamp(hh0+dirY*dy,min,1);
        let w=Math.abs(wC-w0)/Math.max(min,w0)>=Math.abs(hC-hh0)/Math.max(min,hh0)?wC:hC*wanted;
        let height=w/wanted;
        if(height>1){height=1;w=height*wanted;}if(w>1){w=1;height=w/wanted;}
        if(dirX<0)c.left=1-s.right-w;else c.right=1-s.left-w;
        if(dirY<0)c.top=1-s.bottom-height;else c.bottom=1-s.top-height;
      }
    }
    return normalizeCrop(c);
  }
  function cropPaint(){
    const d=cdrag;if(!d)return;
    d.raf=0;
    const next=cropCompute(d);
    d.ctx.clip.crop=next;
    const r=frameRect(d.ctx,next);
    writeFrame(d.ctx,r);
    paintOverlay(d.ctx,r,next,d.M,d.k,false,d.ratio);
  }
  function cropEnd(e){
    const d=cdrag;if(!d)return;
    cdrag=null;
    if(d.raf){cancelAnimationFrame(d.raf);d.raf=0;}
    if(e&&e.type==='pointerup'&&Number.isFinite(e.clientX)){d.cx=e.clientX;d.cy=e.clientY;}
    try{
      const next=cropCompute(d);d.ctx.clip.crop=next;
      const r=frameRect(d.ctx,next);writeFrame(d.ctx,r);
      paintOverlay(d.ctx,r,next,d.M,d.k,false,d.ratio);
    }catch(_e){}
    clearHot();
    window.__ev176CropBusy=false;
    lastCropEnd=now();
    try{d.ctx.stage.releasePointerCapture(d.id);}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}
  }
  function cropPointer(e){
    const t=e.type;
    if(cdrag){
      if(e.pointerId!==cdrag.id)return true;                      // ignore extra fingers during a crop drag
      if(t==='pointermove'){
        cdrag.cx=e.clientX;cdrag.cy=e.clientY;
        if(!cdrag.moved){if(Math.hypot(cdrag.cx-cdrag.x0,cdrag.cy-cdrag.y0)<1)return true;cdrag.moved=true;}
        if(!cdrag.raf)cdrag.raf=requestAnimationFrame(cropPaint);
        if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
        return true;
      }
      if(t==='pointerup'||t==='pointercancel'||t==='lostpointercapture'){cropEnd(e);return true;}
      return true;
    }
    if(t!=='pointerdown'||!cropActive())return false;
    if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return false;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return false;
    const sr=stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(e.clientX<sr.left||e.clientX>sr.right||e.clientY<sr.top||e.clientY>sr.bottom)return false;
    if(interactive(e.target))return false;
    return cropStart(e);
  }
  function cropTouch(e){
    const type=e.type;
    if(cdrag){if(type==='touchmove'||type==='touchstart'){if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();return true;}return false;}
    if(type!=='touchstart'||!cropActive())return false;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!stage.contains(e.target)||interactive(e.target))return false;
    if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
    return true;
  }
  function cropClick(e){
    if(!lastCropEnd||now()-lastCropEnd>400)return false;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');
    return !!(stage&&stage.contains(e.target)&&!interactive(e.target));
  }
  // Safety: never leave the renderer paused.
  window.addEventListener('blur',()=>{if(cdrag)cropEnd(null);if(drag)finishSlider();});
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden){if(cdrag)cropEnd(null);if(drag)finishSlider();}});

  /* =====================================================================
     C. ONE REGISTRATION POINT (runs before every older handler)
     ===================================================================== */
  const opt={capture:true,passive:false};
  function claim(fn,e){let r=false;try{r=fn(e);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v176 gate:',err);}if(r)e.stopImmediatePropagation();return r;}
  ['pointerdown','pointermove','pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'].forEach(type=>{
    window.addEventListener(type,e=>{if(!claim(cropPointer,e))claim(sliderPointer,e);},opt);
  });
  ['touchstart','touchmove','touchend','touchcancel'].forEach(type=>{
    window.addEventListener(type,e=>{if(!claim(cropTouch,e))claim(sliderTouch,e);},opt);
  });
  window.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{claim(sliderMouse,e);},opt);
  window.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(!claim(cropClick,e))claim(sliderMouse,e);},opt);
  window.addEventListener('input',e=>{claim(sliderValue,e);},opt);
  window.addEventListener('change',e=>{claim(sliderValue,e);},opt);
  // Safety net: whenever a touch ends / is cancelled and no crop drag is running, nothing may stay blue.
  ['pointerup','pointercancel','touchend','touchcancel','lostpointercapture'].forEach(type=>{
    window.addEventListener(type,()=>{if(!cdrag)clearHot();},{capture:true,passive:true});
  });

  /* =====================================================================
     D. HOUSEKEEPING (needs the DOM / the app's globals, so it runs later)
     ===================================================================== */
  function installRenderGuard(){
    const f=window.renderFrame;
    if(typeof f!=='function'||f.__ev176Guard)return;
    const guarded=function(){if(window.__ev176CropBusy)return;return f.apply(this,arguments);};
    try{Object.keys(f).forEach(key=>{guarded[key]=f[key];});}catch(_e){}
    guarded.__ev176Guard=true;
    window.renderFrame=guarded;
  }
  function watchCrop(){
    const scr=$('screen-editor');if(!scr)return;
    let frameObs=null,sizeObs=null,active=false,tries=0;
    const attach=()=>{
      const frame=$('evV89CropFrame'),stage=$('canvasStage');
      if(!frame){if(++tries<40)requestAnimationFrame(attach);return;}
      if(frameObs)frameObs.disconnect();
      frameObs=new MutationObserver(scheduleRefresh);
      frameObs.observe(frame,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['style']});
      if(sizeObs)sizeObs.disconnect();
      if(stage&&typeof ResizeObserver!=='undefined'){sizeObs=new ResizeObserver(scheduleRefresh);sizeObs.observe(stage);}
      scheduleRefresh();
    };
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      const on=scr.classList.contains('ev-v89-crop-active');
      if(on===active)return;
      active=on;
      if(on){tries=0;requestAnimationFrame(attach);}
      else{
        if(frameObs){frameObs.disconnect();frameObs=null;}
        if(sizeObs){sizeObs.disconnect();sizeObs=null;}
        clearHot();removeLayer();window.__ev176CropBusy=false;cdrag=null;
      }
    }).observe(scr,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  function boot(){installRenderGuard();watchCrop();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  window.addEventListener('load',installRenderGuard,{once:true});
  setTimeout(installRenderGuard,1500);

  G.eligible=eligible;
})();
</script>

    display:block; overflow:visible; padding:0 2px 2px 0; box-sizing:border-box;

  /* FIXED ("profile photo cutted its corner and showed a default image on background"):
     .profile-avatar is display:flex, and this <img> had no position of its own — as a plain
     flex child it was centered at its natural aspect-fit size instead of being stretched to
     fill the circle, so the gradient + "U" fallback behind it peeked out around the edges.
     position:absolute + inset:0 takes it out of flex flow entirely so it always fills the
     full circle, corner to corner, regardless of the image's own aspect ratio. */
  .profile-avatar img{ position:absolute; inset:0; width:100%; height:100%; object-fit:cover; border-radius:50%; display:block; }

  /* Background image, kept separate from the canvas-stage's own CSS `background` so it can
     be panned/zoomed with a transform (a plain `background` shorthand can't be nudged like
     this). Sized 1px larger than the stage on all sides at scale 1 so a small pan never
     reveals a seam at the edge before the user has zoomed in. */
  /* UPDATED ("if the background didn't fit my project's ratio it cut the image — I want it NOT
     cut or cropped"): this used `object-fit:cover`, which scales the picture up until it covers
     the whole stage and then throws away whatever hangs over the edges — exactly the cropping
     complained about (e.g. a portrait photo on a 16:9 stage lost its top and bottom). It is now
     `object-fit:contain`: the WHOLE image is always visible, scaled to the largest size that fits
     inside the stage without cutting any of it. Any leftover space (an image whose ratio differs
     from the project's) simply shows the stage's own background colour behind it instead of
     removing part of the picture. The user can still zoom in on purpose with the on-canvas
     box / Transform panel (scale > 100%) to frame just a part of the image. The extra 1px bleed
     that used to hide a hairline seam under `cover` is no longer needed and is removed, since it
     would now nudge a `contain`-fitted image 1px out of its true position. */
  .bg-img-layer{
    position:absolute; top:0; left:0; width:100%; height:100%;
    object-fit:contain; object-position:center; transform-origin:center center;
    pointer-events:none; display:none; will-change:transform;
  }

  /* Fixed center playhead: sticky on BOTH axes so it stays glued to the visual center
     and full height of the visible scroller no matter how far the user scrolls
     horizontally (through time) or vertically (through layers). Track content scrolls
     underneath it instead (CapCut-style). Not draggable itself. Sticky-on-both-axes is
     what keeps it fully visible and stops it drifting/jumping during scroll. */
  .playhead-wrap{
    position:sticky; top:0; left:50%; width:0; height:0; pointer-events:none; z-index:12;
    float:left;
  }
  .playhead{
    position:sticky; top:0; width:2px; background:var(--accent); left:0; margin-left:-1px;
    pointer-events:none; display:block;
    /* height is set inline by JS (syncPlayheadHeight) to exactly match the visible
       scroller height, so the line always spans edge-to-edge and never gets clipped
       or appears to "float" partway down when layers are added/removed.
       No decorative cap/pill on top — just a clean vertical line, CapCut-style. */
  }
  /* BUGFIX (selected clip's RIGHT trim handle invisible/cut off near a track's full width,
     while the LEFT handle always rendered fine): .layer-track used to carry its own
     `margin-right:12px` for the visual gap before the next thing in the row (the delete
     button on a character row, or just breathing room before the row's own edge otherwise).
     Clip elements inside a track are positioned with `left`/`width` computed purely from
     pxPerSecond()*seconds, sized to fit exactly `timelineDuration*pps` px — which is only
     correct if the track itself is exactly that wide. The 12px margin (and, in an earlier
     attempt at this fix, moving that same 12px to .layer-row's own padding-right instead)
     both shrank the track's box by 12px versus what the clip math assumes, and since
     .layer-track also has overflow:hidden, a clip's rightmost ~12px — almost exactly the
     width of the 20px-wide right trim handle — was silently clipped off by the track's own
     boundary whenever trimEnd approached the full timeline duration. The left handle,
     anchored at the track's own left:0, was never affected by a RIGHT-side gap. FIX: the
     12px gap no longer touches .layer-row or .layer-track's box at all — it now lives as
     margin-left on .char-row-delete only (the one element that actually needs breathing
     room from the track before it), so the track is always exactly timelineDuration*pps
     wide with nothing subtracted that the clip math doesn't already account for. */
  /* BUGFIX ("an extended Animation clip can go under/over another row" — e.g. after
     speeding a clip up/down or dragging its handle so it grows past its row's original
     height): every clip (.anim-clip/.overlay-clip/.track-clip) sets its OWN z-index
     (3/6/10…) so drag/selection can visually win over idle neighbors — but those z-index
     values were being compared in the shared stacking context of the whole timeline
     (#timelineInner), not scoped to each clip's own row. Nothing here ever actually
     established .layer-row as its own stacking context, so a clip with a momentarily higher
     z-index than normal (mid-drag, or just-selected — see .anim-clip.dragging/.selected)
     could paint on top of a DIFFERENT row's content if that row happened to sit adjacent in
     the DOM and its own box hadn't finished growing to the new clip height yet on that
     render frame — reading exactly as "the clip went under/over the row". `isolation:
     isolate` makes each row its own self-contained stacking context: every z-index on a
     clip inside a row is now only ever compared against OTHER clips in that SAME row, and
     can never climb above or below a sibling row's content no matter what value it has.
     `overflow:hidden` on the row itself is a second, independent guarantee: even the row's
     own .layer-track already clips its clips (see .layer-track's own overflow:hidden) — this
     adds a matching backstop at the row level, so even the layer-badge/padding area of the
     row can never let a clip bleed past the row's own top/bottom edge either. */
  .layer-row{
    display:flex; align-items:center; height:64px; min-height:64px; max-height:64px;
    flex-shrink:0; border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.04);
    padding:0 0 0 12px; gap:10px; position:relative; box-sizing:border-box;
    isolation:isolate; overflow:hidden;
  }
  /* Clips/Audio group rows (see packOverlayGroup) can grow taller than the fixed 64px when
     more than one sub-lane is needed for genuinely time-overlapping clips — the row's own
     inline height (set per-render from however many sub-lanes are in use) must win over the
     fixed default above, so drop the min/max clamp specifically for these two rows. */
  .layer-row.overlay-track-row{ height:auto; min-height:64px; max-height:none; align-items:flex-start; padding-top:0; }
  .layer-row.overlay-track-row .layer-badge{ margin-top:19px; }
  /* Character rows grow to fit their own main + split-clip overlap lanes. The final
     v112 lane-height rules at the end of the document override the legacy fixed sizing. */
  .layer-row.char-row{ height:auto; min-height:64px; max-height:none; align-items:flex-start; padding-top:0; }
  .layer-row.char-row .layer-badge{ margin-top:19px; }
  /* .char-row-track's own height override used to live here — see its new location just
     after `.layer-track`'s own rule below for why it had to move (source-order cascade
     bug: this and `.layer-track` are equal-specificity class selectors, and CSS resolves
     ties by whichever rule comes LAST in the stylesheet, not which one "sounds like" an
     override. With this rule sitting BEFORE `.layer-track`, `.layer-track`'s later
     `max-height:60px` was silently winning and clamping every character row's track back
     down to 60px regardless of this rule's `max-height:none` — see the full bugfix note by
     `.layer-track` for the on-device symptom this caused). */
  /* BUGFIX (Background row rendered visibly shorter than the Clips/Audio rows on a brand
     new project): the empty Clips/Audio placeholder row is SUB_LANE_H(48) + 12 = 60px tall
     for its .layer-track, sitting inside a 72px .layer-row (see renderOverlayLayers). The
     Background row's .layer-track was hard-pinned to a fixed 52px (height/min-height/
     max-height all 52px, inside the also-fixed 64px .layer-row) with nothing tying the two
     numbers together, so the two rows simply didn't match — Background looked "shorter" the
     instant a Clips/Audio row was empty (its dashed-placeholder height) or on screen at the
     same time. Both rows now share the exact same 60px track / 72px row sizing so every
     track row in the timeline reads as the same height regardless of type. */
  .layer-row[data-layer="background"]{ height:72px; min-height:72px; max-height:72px; }
  /* BUGFIX ("border like in Background row short and only covers little bit area"): the
     Background row's own #track-background container spans the row's full width exactly
     like every other row (it's a normal .layer-track), but it never drew any border of its
     own — only the #bgTrackClip BOX sitting inside it (sized to bgDuration, often much
     shorter than the full row) had a visible border. That made a short background clip
     look like the whole row's "border" was short, instead of reading as one full-width
     track with a shorter clip placed on it — unlike the dashed "Add character"/"Add
     overlay" placeholders, which visually fill their entire row because they have no inner
     sized box at all. A faint dashed baseline the full width of the track (BEHIND the real
     clip box, and click/drag-through via pointer-events:none) now shows the row's true
     full extent at a glance, the same way the placeholder rows already do. */
  /* UPDATED ("background blocks are not covering the Background row widely — fix it and adjust
     its size for covering properly"): this track used to draw its dashed lane outline as a real
     `border:1.5px dashed`. A CSS border eats into the box, so the absolutely-positioned clips
     inside it were laid out against a padding box that was 3px smaller than the row, and the clip
     itself was ALSO inset a further 3px top and bottom (top:3px; height:54px inside a 60px
     track). Together that left a visible ~4-5px gap of empty dashed track above, below and to the
     left of every background block, so the block never looked like it filled the row (compare the
     character clip beneath it, which fills its lane). The outline is now painted by ::after as
     a pure overlay (pointer-events:none, above the clip's z-order but only 1.5px thick at the very
     edge) so it takes ZERO layout space: the clip's containing box is exactly the track's full
     60px x full-width, and the block below is sized to cover that whole box edge to edge. */
  #track-background{ box-sizing:border-box; }
  #track-background::after{
    /* explicit top/left/width/height (not inset/bottom): see the Android-WebView note on .track-clip */
    content:''; position:absolute; top:0; left:0; width:100%; height:100%; box-sizing:border-box;
    border:1.5px dashed var(--line); border-radius:6px;
    pointer-events:none; z-index:1;
  }
  /* Real clips sit ABOVE the dashed outline so the outline never shows through a covering block.
     Square corners in this lane (the generic .track-clip has 6px rounding): with rounded ends,
     two back-to-back backgrounds left a small dark notch of empty lane at the top and bottom of
     every seam, which read as the block "not covering the row". Square ends butt up flush. */
  /* UPDATED ("left side round but right side sharp, and cut/split background pieces show sharp
     square corners — make every side round like the animation clips"): this rule used to force
     border-radius:0 on every Background clip. The row's own .layer-track has a 6px radius +
     overflow:hidden, so the FIRST clip's LEFT end still looked round (the track was rounding it,
     not the clip) while its RIGHT end and both ends of every cut piece were square. Every
     Background clip now carries its own 6px radius on all four corners, selected or not — the
     exact same rounding .anim-clip / .overlay-clip use — so left/right ends and each half of a
     split all read as rounded pills. The selected-state white handle bars and top/bottom edge
     bars sit inside the clip's overflow:hidden box, so they are rounded by it automatically. */
  #track-background .track-clip,
  #track-background .track-clip.selected{ z-index:2; border-radius:6px; }
  .layer-row.selected{ background:rgba(47,92,232,0.06); }
  .char-row.selected{ background:transparent; box-shadow:none; }
  /* Character-side glyph is a passive row icon, never a separately selectable object.
     Keep it out of pointer targeting and do not draw the white clip-selection outline around it;
     the row/clip and the bottom toolbar retain their normal selection behavior. */
  .char-row .layer-badge{ pointer-events:none; cursor:default; outline:none !important; box-shadow:none !important; }
  .char-row.chrome-selected .layer-badge{ outline:none !important; }
  /* CapCut-style layer icon badge — replaces the old flat color dot. Shows a small
     glyph identifying the layer TYPE (rig/character, image, video, audio, text) rather
     than an arbitrary color swatch.
     BUGFIX (badge didn't slide with the row on horizontal scroll): this used to be
     `position:sticky; left:0`, which pins the badge to the SCROLLER's own left edge —
     since .timeline-scroll is the one shared scroll container for both the ruler/tracks
     (horizontal, time) AND the layer list (vertical), a sticky-left badge stays glued to
     the screen while the row's own track content slides underneath it, instead of moving
     together as one row like every other timeline editor does. The badge is now a plain
     in-flow flex child again (no position/left/z-index of its own) so it scrolls exactly
     in lockstep with its row — z-index:11 is dropped too since nothing needs to stack
     above the scrolling content anymore now that it isn't pinned. */
  .layer-badge{
    width:26px; height:26px; border-radius:8px; flex-shrink:0; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;
    background:var(--elevated-2); border:1.5px solid var(--line); color:var(--text-dim);
  }
  .layer-badge svg{ width:14px; height:14px; }
  .overlay-track-row .layer-track,.overlay-clip{will-change:transform;}
  .layer-row.selected .layer-badge{ border-color:var(--accent); color:var(--accent); background:rgba(47,92,232,0.12); box-shadow:0 0 0 1px rgba(47,92,232,.22),0 0 12px rgba(47,92,232,.28); }
  .layer-row.selected .layer-badge svg,#bgLayerBadge.active svg{filter:drop-shadow(0 0 4px rgba(69,116,255,.95));stroke:#78A0FF!important;}
  #bgLayerBadge.active{border-color:var(--accent)!important;color:#78A0FF!important;background:rgba(47,92,232,.16)!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px rgba(47,92,232,.22),0 0 12px rgba(47,92,232,.3)!important;}
  .layer-track{
    flex:1 0 auto; height:60px; min-height:60px; max-height:60px;
    background:linear-gradient(180deg, #17171B, #131316);
    position:relative; overflow:hidden; border-radius:6px; box-sizing:border-box;
    contain:layout size; /* stops the flex parent's reflow timing from ever shrinking this before children (the overlay clip) commit their own geometry — see .overlay-clip bugfix note */
  }
  /* Clips/Audio group track: height comes entirely from inline style (packOverlayGroup sub-
     lane count) — remove the fixed clamp so it can actually grow/shrink between renders. */
  .overlay-track-row .layer-track{ height:auto; min-height:60px; max-height:none; margin-top:6px; margin-bottom:6px; }
  /* BUGFIX ("Animation/layer clips slide down under the row, bottom corners look cut off" —
     confirmed live: with the old rule order, a character row's track computed to a hard
     60px tall — getComputedStyle showed max-height/height BOTH clamped at 60px — even though
     renderTimelineClip()/applyCharClipLanes() were setting an inline `height:64px` on this
     exact element every render. Root cause: `.char-row-track`'s own `max-height:none`
     override used to be declared BEFORE this `.layer-track` rule in the stylesheet. Browsers
     resolve a tie between two equal-specificity class selectors (both are just one class,
     0-0-1-0) by whichever rule appears LAST in source order — so this rule's own
     `max-height:60px`, coming after, was winning regardless of the earlier "override" and
     silently clamping every character row's track back to 60px. A clip positioned at
     top:3px + height:58px (=61px, meant to fit inside the real 64px track with 3px to
     spare top AND bottom) instead had its bottom 1px+ sliced off by this too-short track's
     own overflow:hidden — reading exactly as "clip sits low, bottom corner cut off".
     FIX: move `.char-row-track`'s override to right here, immediately after this rule, so
     it's unambiguously LAST and always wins the tie — and give it an explicit numeric
     max-height (64px, matching charRowHeightForLanes's real single-lane content height)
     instead of `none`, so this can never again silently mis-clamp if a future rule gets
     inserted between the two. */
  .char-row-track{ height:60px; min-height:60px; max-height:60px; margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:2px; }
  /* Character layer rows (see renderCharacterLayers) — one per character, tap to select.

       middle): .anim-clip-handle (the 20px edge grips) and .anim-clip-body both explicitly
       set touch-action:none, but this element — the actual hit target under the whole
       middle of the clip, since .clip-thumb-strip/.clip-media-label are position:absolute;
       inset:0 and sit on top of it — never did. With no touch-action:none here, a
       long-press-then-drag starting anywhere over the middle got hijacked by the browser's
       own default touch handling (treated as a scroll/pan gesture) instead of reaching
       wireBackgroundTrackInteractions' pointerdown/pointermove sequence, so only the edges
       (already covered by their own touch-action:none) actually dragged. */
    touch-action:none;
    /* BUGFIX ("selected clip only shows white border on left/right, not top/bottom" — the
       Background/uploaded-clip box, on-device, still on Android WebView): this element used
       to size itself purely from top:3px/bottom:3px against its parent .layer-track (60px).
       That's the EXACT same fragile pattern .overlay-clip's own BUGFIX note above already
       documents and fixes: a `bottom` offset only resolves once the browser has fully
       committed the parent's height for that layout pass, and on this WebView a re-render
       that only flips the `.selected` class (no other layout change) doesn't reliably
       trigger that — so the box's real height was landing wrong on selection, squeezing the
       inset box-shadow ring's top/bottom edges down to nothing while the left/right ::before/
       ::after bars (fixed-width blocks, not dependent on the box's resolved height) kept
       showing fine. Same fix as .overlay-clip: pin an explicit height directly on the element
       itself (54px = the parent .layer-track's 60px minus the 3px+3px top/bottom gutter this
       rule always intended), so the box's own height is authoritative and immune to that
       parent-resolution race, on every clip type that uses .track-clip (Background AND any
       plain uploaded clip rendered through it). */
  }
  /* Empty-state Background clip (no image/color chosen yet) — same dashed-placeholder

     SEL_EDGE_HTML), not `outline` or `box-shadow`: on at least one real Android WebView

     element's own `overflow:hidden` + border-radius inside the timeline's scrolling/
     composited ancestor. Four independent bars can't be selectively clipped the way a single
     ring effect can.
     Using a dedicated sel-edge-left/right sibling (instead of styling .anim-clip-handle's own
     ::before, which centers itself via flex) is what guarantees this bar starts flush at the

  /* ---- Custom character builder: 10-part upload grid + pivot editor ---- */
  /* BUGFIX ("part-upload blocks rendering under/overlapping each other"): the previous version
     relied on aspect-ratio:1 to size each .part-slot, which on a narrow phone width computes a
     slot height independent of the grid's actual row height inside this height-constrained
     flex sheet — rows then overlap instead of stacking cleanly. Fixed by sizing rows explicitly
     with grid-auto-rows:minmax(100px,auto) on the grid and min-height:100px/height:100% (no
     aspect-ratio) on each .part-slot, so every slot fills its own real grid cell no matter the
     screen width. The builder modal's sheet still gets a definite height:82vh + overflow:hidden
     inline (see the modal markup) so the sheet's own box is unambiguous on every engine, and
     this grid still scrolls internally via its own overflow-y:auto (set inline on the element). */
  .part-slot-grid{
    display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr)); grid-auto-rows:minmax(100px,auto);
    gap:10px; align-items:stretch; justify-items:stretch;
    min-height:0; flex:1 1 0; width:100%; box-sizing:border-box;
    overflow-x:hidden;
  }

  /* On/off toggle switch that actually APPLIES the previewed animation to the selected clip
     — tapping the row itself now only PREVIEWS a preset (poses/plays it on canvas without
     touching the clip's saved animKey), matching the request that presets stop auto-applying
     the instant they're tapped. This switch is the deliberate second, separate action that
     commits the choice. Only one preset can be "on" at a time (they drive the same clip), so
     flipping one on flips any other off — same semantics as a radio group, styled as a
     switch per item since each row needs its own visible on/off state. */

<script id="ethvelle-v258-early-helpers">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* ---- Timeline character icon follows the model type (same line style for all three) ---- */
  var ICONS={
    humanoid:'<circle cx="12" cy="6" r="3"/><path d="M12 9v6M12 12l-5 4M12 12l5 3M12 15l-4 6M12 15l4 6"/>',
    animal:'<circle cx="11" cy="4" r="2"/><circle cx="18" cy="8" r="2"/><circle cx="20" cy="16" r="2"/><path d="M9 10a5 5 0 0 1 5 5v3.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-6.84 1.045Q6.52 17.48 4.46 16.84A3.5 3.5 0 0 1 5.5 10Z"/>',
    beast:'<path d=\"M5 3.5 9 7h6l4-3.5V11a7 7 0 0 1-14 0z\"/><path d=\"M10 13.5h4l-2 2z\"/><path d=\"M9 11h.01M15 11h.01\"/>',
    bird:'<path d="M16 7h.01"/><path d="M3.4 18H12a8 8 0 0 0 8-8V7a4 4 0 0 0-7.28-2.3L2 20"/><path d="m20 7 2 .5-2 .5"/><path d="M10 18v3"/><path d="M14 17.75V21"/><path d="M7 18a6 6 0 0 0 3.84-10.61"/>',
    mermaid:'<circle cx="12" cy="3.6" r="2.1"/><path d="M9.5 6.2c-1.5.9-2.5 2.5-2.5 4.3v1.1c0 1.2.9 2.1 2.1 2.1h5.8c1.2 0 2.1-.9 2.1-2.1v-1.1c0-1.8-1-3.4-2.5-4.3"/><path d="M9.1 8.4 7.3 10.1M14.9 8.4l1.8 1.7"/><path d="M10 13.7c-.1 1.3-.9 2.1-1.9 3.1-1.2 1.2-.7 2.8.8 3.4 1.2.5 2.3.9 3.1 2 .8-1.1 1.9-1.5 3.1-2 1.5-.6 2-2.2.8-3.4-1-1-1.8-1.8-1.9-3.1"/><path d="M12 22.1c-.9-1.2-2.2-1.8-4.2-1.8.7-1.1 1.4-2 2.5-2.6M12 22.1c.9-1.2 2.2-1.8 4.2-1.8-.7-1.1-1.4-2-2.5-2.6"/>',
    lamia:'<path d="M4.5 20c0-4.1 2.6-7.2 6.1-7.2 2.3 0 3.3 1.8 5.5 1.8 2.4 0 3.9-1.5 3.9-3.7 0-1.9-1.3-3.3-3.1-3.3-1.7 0-3 1.2-3 2.8"/><path d="m16.4 7.7 1.5-2.1 2.8.5-.5 2.8-2.3.8"/><path d="m20.1 8.4 1.7-.9m-1.7.9 1.2 1.4"/><circle cx="18.7" cy="7.2" r=".45" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/><path d="M6.2 17.4h.01M8.7 15.4h.01M11.2 15.4h.01"/>'
  };  window.evCharModelType=function(c){
    var t=String(c&&(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid');
    if(c&&((c.sheet&&c.sheet.beast)||c.beast))return 'beast';
    return ['animal','bird','mermaid','lamia'].includes(t)?t:'humanoid';
  };
  window.evCharBadgeIconSvg=function(type){
    type=ICONS[type]?type:'humanoid';
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" data-char-icon="'+type+'">'+ICONS[type]+'</svg>';
  };

  /* ---- Animation search: keep the canvas at its original size while the keyboard is open ---- */
  var SEL='#evcaAnimSearchInput,#clipAnimationSearchInput';
  var locked=false,baseVisible=0,releaseTimer=0;
  function visibleH(){var vv=window.visualViewport;return vv?vv.height:window.innerHeight;}
  function isSearch(el){return !!(el&&el.matches&&el.matches(SEL));}
  function lock(){
    if(releaseTimer){clearTimeout(releaseTimer);releaseTimer=0;}
    if(locked)return;
    var wrap=document.querySelector('#screen-editor .canvas-wrap');
    if(!wrap)return;
    var h=wrap.getBoundingClientRect().height;
    if(!(h>0))return;
    baseVisible=visibleH();
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--ev-kb-wrap-h',Math.round(h)+'px');
    document.body.classList.add('ev-kb-lock');
    locked=true;
  }
  function unlock(){
    if(releaseTimer){clearTimeout(releaseTimer);releaseTimer=0;}
    if(!locked)return;
    locked=false;
    document.body.classList.remove('ev-kb-lock');
    document.documentElement.style.removeProperty('--ev-kb-wrap-h');
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){try{window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));}catch(e){}});
  }
  document.addEventListener('focusin',function(e){if(isSearch(e.target))lock();},true);
  document.addEventListener('focusout',function(e){
    if(!isSearch(e.target))return;
    if(releaseTimer)clearTimeout(releaseTimer);
    releaseTimer=setTimeout(function(){releaseTimer=0;if(!isSearch(document.activeElement))unlock();},350);
  },true);
  function guard(e){
    if(!locked)return;
    if(visibleH()>=baseVisible-2){unlock();return;}   /* keyboard is gone: allow the normal re-fit */
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();                      /* keyboard is up: do not re-fit / shrink the canvas */
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',guard,true);
  if(window.visualViewport){
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',guard,true);
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',guard,true);
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v265-highres-logo-binder">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var splash=document.getElementById('splashLogo');
  var source=document.getElementById('splashLogoImg');
  if(!source)return;
  // Keep the logo externally hosted as requested; never inline/base64-embed this artwork.
  var uri='https://i.ibb.co.com/pBy7qxjG/file-0000000097ac8211b2cf9825ab2e845f.png';
  source.onerror=function(){if(splash)splash.classList.add('logo-fallback');};
  source.src=uri;source.setAttribute('src',uri);
  source.decoding='async';source.loading='eager';source.fetchPriority='high';
  if(splash)splash.style.setProperty('--splash-logo-src','url("'+uri+'")');
  function applyBrandLogo(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.admin-login-logo,.home-brand-logo-only,.ev-login-consent-mark img').forEach(function(img){
      if(img===source)return;
      img.onerror=null;
      img.src=uri;img.setAttribute('src',uri);
      img.decoding='async';img.loading='eager';
    });
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',applyBrandLogo,{once:true});
  else applyBrandLogo();
})();
</script>
