  <!-- ============ PRIVACY POLICY ============ -->
  <div class="screen" id="screen-privacy">
    <div class="sub-topbar">
      <button type="button" class="sub-back-btn" onclick="returnFromEthvelleLegalPage()" aria-label="Back"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6"/></svg></button>
      <h1>Privacy policy</h1>
    </div>
    <div class="demo-scroll legal-scroll">
      <article class="legal-document">
        <h2>How Ethvelle handles your information</h2>
        <p class="legal-updated">Effective date: 7 November 2026</p>
        <p>This Privacy Policy explains what information Ethvelle processes when you use the Ethvelle animation studio, website, account, payment and support features. We aim to collect only information needed to operate the service, maintain accounts, verify orders, protect the platform and respond to support requests.</p>
        <section><h3>1. Information we process</h3>
          <ul>
            <li><b>Account and profile information:</b> email address and sign-in identity, plus profile details you choose to enter such as name, username, headline, biography and profile image.</li>
            <li><b>Orders and payment verification:</b> selected plan, payment method, transaction/reference ID and the payment-proof image(s) you submit. Ethvelle currently provides manual bKash/Nagad payment verification in the Bangladesh checkout flow.</li>
            <li><b>Usage and security events:</b> randomly generated visitor/session identifiers and service events such as screen visits, plan interactions, project/model actions and export activity. These help us understand usage, protect the service and improve reliability.</li>
            <li><b>Projects and media on your device:</b> project records, animation work, character/model data and imported images, video and audio may be stored in your browser's local storage or IndexedDB on the device you are using. This is device storage and is not automatically a cloud backup or cross-device sync.</li>
            <li><b>Support messages:</b> information you include when contacting support through the contact options shown in the app.</li>
          </ul>
        </section>
        <section><h3>2. How we use information</h3>
          <p>We use information to create and secure accounts; provide editor, project, plan and support features; verify payments and activate eligible plans; maintain order and service records; investigate errors or misuse; measure general product usage; and comply with legal obligations.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>3. Where information is stored and service providers</h3>
          <p>Ethvelle uses Firebase Authentication for account sign-in and Firebase/Firestore services for selected account, order, site-content and service records. Payment-proof images and order details are submitted for manual verification. Projects and imported media are also stored in your browser's local storage or IndexedDB when supported by your browser. The exact data stored in each place depends on the feature you use.</p>
          <p>We may rely on infrastructure and technology providers needed to operate authentication, database, fonts, hosted media and embedded tutorial content. Those providers may process technical information under their own terms and privacy notices. We do not sell your personal information.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>4. When information may be shared</h3>
          <p>We may make relevant information available to authorised Ethvelle personnel or payment reviewers who need it to verify an order or provide support; to service providers acting on our instructions; when required by law or a lawful request; or when reasonably necessary to protect users, the service or legal rights. We do not publish your private project files as public content merely because you use the editor.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>5. Device storage, backups and deletion</h3>
          <p>Projects and imported media saved in browser storage may be lost if you clear site data, uninstall/reset the browser, change devices or use a browser that does not support the storage feature. Export important work and keep your own backup. Removing a project inside the editor may not remove account, order or transaction records that must be retained for operational or legal reasons.</p>
          <p>You can request access to, correction of or deletion of personal information, or ask a question about its use, by emailing <a class="ev-support-email-link" href="mailto:support@ethvelle.com">support@ethvelle.com</a>. We may need to verify your identity and may retain information where the law permits or requires it.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>6. Security and retention</h3>
          <p>We use reasonable technical and organisational safeguards appropriate to the information and service. No website, device or transmission method can be guaranteed completely secure. We keep information for as long as reasonably necessary for the purposes described above, account and order administration, dispute handling, security and applicable legal requirements.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>7. Your choices and rights</h3>
          <p>You may update profile details through available settings, control whether you continue using optional features, and contact us to request access, correction or deletion where applicable. Where processing relies on consent, you may request to withdraw it; this does not affect processing already lawfully completed or processing that has another lawful basis. Requests are handled subject to identity checks and applicable law.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>8. Children and sensitive information</h3>
          <p>Do not submit sensitive personal information through profile fields, project names or support messages unless it is necessary and you have a lawful reason to do so. Anyone under 18 should use the service with the involvement and guidance of a parent or legal guardian.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>9. Changes and contact</h3>
          <p>We may update this Policy when the service, its data practices or applicable requirements change. The latest version and effective date will be shown on this page. For privacy questions or requests, contact <a class="ev-support-email-link" href="mailto:support@ethvelle.com">support@ethvelle.com</a>.</p>
        </section>
        <p class="legal-footnote">This notice is intended to describe the current Ethvelle website implementation. It does not limit rights that cannot lawfully be limited under applicable law.</p>
      </article>
    </div>
  </div>

        <h2>Terms for using Ethvelle</h2>
        <p class="legal-updated">Effective date: 7 November 2026</p>
        <p>These Terms &amp; Conditions govern your access to and use of the Ethvelle website, animation editor, account, projects, plans and related features. By accessing or using Ethvelle, you agree to these Terms. Do not use the service if you do not agree.</p>
        <section><h3>1. Accounts and eligibility</h3>
          <p>Provide accurate account and payment details, keep your sign-in credentials secure and notify us if you suspect unauthorised access. You are responsible for activity carried out through your account, except where applicable law provides otherwise. If you are under 18, use the service with parent or legal-guardian involvement.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>2. Plans, prices and activation</h3>
          <p>Plan features, limits, prices and billing terms are the details shown on the relevant plan and checkout screens at the time you place an order. Do not send payment to a number or account that is not displayed in the official Ethvelle checkout.</p>
        </section>
        <section class="legal-refund"><h3>3. Refunds and cancellations</h3>
          <p><b>Plan purchases are final and non-refundable once the paid plan has been activated and its features, limits or benefits have been made available to your account.</b> A refund is not provided simply because you change your mind, do not use the plan, use fewer exports or projects than included, or decide to stop using the service after activation.</p>
          <p>This policy does not remove any refund, cancellation, remedy or consumer right that cannot legally be excluded. If you are charged more than once, pay the wrong amount because of an Ethvelle checkout error, or a paid plan is not activated or supplied after payment has been verified, contact <a class="ev-support-email-link" href="mailto:support@ethvelle.com">support@ethvelle.com</a> with your order details so the issue can be investigated and corrected or refunded where required by law.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>4. Your projects and uploaded content</h3>
          <p>You remain responsible for the images, video, audio, text, character sheets and other material you import or create. You must own or have permission to use that material and must not upload content that infringes another person's rights or violates applicable law. You grant Ethvelle only the limited permission reasonably needed to process material you submit for the feature you choose, support the service and verify related orders. Project files and imported media may be saved in browser storage on your device; keep independent backups.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>5. Acceptable use</h3>
          <p>Do not attempt to disrupt, reverse engineer, exploit or gain unauthorised access to Ethvelle or its accounts and infrastructure; introduce malware; interfere with another user's work; misuse payment or support flows; or use the service to create, store or distribute unlawful, infringing, deceptive or harmful material.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>6. Availability, changes and support</h3>
          <p>We aim to keep Ethvelle available and improve its tools, but features may be updated, limited or temporarily unavailable for maintenance, security, compatibility or operational reasons. We may revise plans and features for future purchases. We will not intentionally remove rights that applicable law requires us to preserve. Contact <a class="ev-support-email-link" href="mailto:support@ethvelle.com">support@ethvelle.com</a> for account, payment or technical support.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>7. Third-party services</h3>
          <p>Some parts of Ethvelle rely on third-party services such as Firebase authentication/database infrastructure, hosted media, fonts or embedded tutorial providers. Their own terms and privacy notices may apply when you use those services. Ethvelle is not responsible for services or content controlled independently by third parties.</p>
        </section>
        <section><h3>8. Suspension and termination</h3>
          <p>We may restrict or suspend access where reasonably necessary to protect the service, users, payment verification or legal compliance, including in response to serious or repeated breaches of these Terms. Where appropriate, we will provide notice and an opportunity to resolve the issue. Suspension does not remove rights that cannot legally be excluded.</p>
        </section>
